# Foldie Project — Brombacher parts (Korean shop) and Gemini / Sixweel

Added 25 Oct 2026 for all three builds (C Line, G Line / T20, Aceoffix). Prices are USD x 1.30 = S$, before shipping from Korea, customs and GST. About half of the weights are estimates (`est.`), basis shown. Fit tags in the details list. Also browsable in the Foldie Build page (Parts book).


## Brombacher (Korean shop)

Source: full crawl of every product list on en.brombacher.kr (all brand, Brompton sub-category and Components sub-category lists, ids 1-260, 1,139 products found; 116 dropped as not parts or not relevant: decals, bags, apparel, lights, phone mounts, Sturmey hub-gear/2-speed/3-speed/12-speed items). Product pages read for weights (g) and prices. Crawled 25 Oct 2026. Builds: (A) Brompton C Line 16"/349 rim brake, external 7s HG; (B) HEXATI T20 20" thru-axle disc (G Line parts); (C) Aceoffix C5/T1 16" (118 mm rear).
**Prices:** Brombacher prices are USD; S$ = USD x 1.30 (fixed rate). Shipping from Korea (international parcel, 7-14 days) is extra and not included; customs/GST on arrival also extra. Price column is the page price at crawl time (variants/sizes can differ). "sold out" is flagged in Fit notes where the page said so.
**Weights:** `page` = read on the Brombacher product page (first figure on the page; where a page lists several sizes it is the first/primary one, unit pair/set/each from the page wording or title). `~N est.` = no weight on the page; estimate explained in the Basis column. Fit tags are derived from the product title/description wording and are NOT verified: "Aceoffix (Brompton-clone, verify)" means a generic Brompton A/C-line part that likely fits the clone but needs measuring; the Aceoffix has 118 mm rear spacing, so rear wheels/hubs may need spacers.
**Gemini and Sixwheels**
- **Gemini:** no product on en.brombacher.kr is branded or described as Gemini (searched the site search for "gemini", all 1,139 product names and descriptions). A web search also finds no Gemini Brompton parts; Gemini (Gemini Composites / Gemini Carbon, Barcelona) makes road/gravel seatposts, none for Brompton, and carbon seatposts are excluded for this build anyway (child seat).
- **Sixwheels / Six Wheels:** zero matches on the site (site search for "sixwheels", "six wheels", "6wheels", "six", plus full text of all product pages) and none on the web search. Closest on Brombacher: Schmolke x Extralite carbon wheelsets and Brombacher x Hubsmith wheels (see Wheels and hubs). Other 349 carbon wheel brands seen in search (not Brombacher): SMC (Lunate, FIVE, Plume), DairsCyclin.
**Count and gaps**
- Every row has a weight and a price. Estimated cells (`~N est.`) are listed with their basis in each row: bolts/washers/nuts computed from dimensions and material density; cogs from ~0.85 g per tooth; chains, tyres, tubes, Shimano shifters, MKS pedals from typical published figures for the same model class (not re-verified at other retailers: time-boxed, so treat as +/-20-30%).
- Variant pages (sizes/colours) show one price; the first weight on the page is used.
- Not catalogued (dropped): Sturmey-Archer/2-speed/3-speed/6-speed/12-speed hub-gear items, decals, bags, apparel, lights, phone mounts, bells, complete bikes and frame sets. The Brompton OEM rows (Brompton brand) are Brombacher resale of genuine spares.
- Brombacher shows no stock level; "sold out" flagged only where the page said so.
- Fit tags unverified; check dimensions (25.4 vs 31.8 bars, 31.6/31.8 posts, 118 mm rear on Aceoffix, G Line 142/135 mm thru-axle/QR) before ordering.

## Gemini Carbon and Sixweel

**Brand identification**
**Gemini Carbon = Gemini (ridegemini.com), Barcelona, Spain.** Forged/UD carbon MTB, road and gravel parts: RIGEL chainrings, ÄLSAK seatposts, WÄSSAT/PRÖPUS/ALZIR handlebars and cockpits, Ögle aero roller. Made by the brothers behind UNNO bikes. No folding-bike, Brompton, 130 BCD or 16"/20" product exists on its site or at any retailer I found. No Singapore stockist found (its site has a Singapore region selector only). Nearest sellers: Anjoy Cycle (Hong Kong), bike-components.de, R2-Bike, Power Meter City, Fair Wheel, Pushys (AU). Alternatives with similar names: none relevant to bikes (Gemini racing car, Google Gemini). Naver, Coupang, AliExpress, Carousell and Shopee SG searches returned nothing for Gemini Brompton parts.
**Sixwheels = most likely "Sixweel" (sixweel.com, also written SIXWEEL / "sixwheel" in URLs).** Brand of "Ti-Master & Sixweel USA LLC" (footer says California; products appear CNC-made, probably in China: contact is a 163.com email, Instagram @sixweelcassettes). Sells titanium road-bike parts: model-specific thru axles, RD hangers, brake caliper adapters, steerer preload, plus the Sixweel Lite 12s HG cassette. Sold in Singapore by tipartstitanium.com (prices already S$) and Chapter2Cycle SG. No Brompton or folding-bike product found. Alternatives: Elitewheels "Six" six-spoke carbon wheels, Black Inc SIXTY, Sixth Element wheels (all unrelated to folders). Brombacher (en.brombacher.kr) brand list (31 brands, read from its Brands page) does NOT contain Gemini or Sixweel; it lists Aceoffix, Ridea, Schmolke, MCFK, Extralite, KCNC, Ti Parts Workshop, Mr TiPARTS and others. The owner may be thinking of Schmolke, MCFK, Ridea or Mr TiPARTS. Not verified which.
**Bottom line: neither brand makes anything that bolts onto A, B or C as sold. The rows below are the only items with any plausible use, each with a fit warning.**
**Count and gaps**
- Chainring 3 rows, handlebar 2, seatpost 2, thru axle 9, cassette 1, hanger/adapter 6. Total 23 rows, none a real fit for A, B or C.
- Estimates: Rigel 40T and 46T weights, Rigel Shimano price (mid), PRÖPUS weight and price, generic Sixweel axle weight, UDH hanger weight, two adapter weights (7 est. cells).
- Not accessible or not found: any Brompton/folding product from either brand; Gemini Singapore stockist; Instagram, Naver, Coupang, AliExpress, Carousell and eBay presence for either brand (searched, nothing found; Instagram not opened); Brombacher product pages for either brand (absent from its brand list); Sixweel factory location; Sixweel axle lengths and thread pitch (not published); Wässat price/weight confirmed only by search-result titles.
- Ambiguity: "Sixwheels" resolved to Sixweel by spelling; if the owner means a different Korean shop item, he needs to send the product link.
